I have completed a read-only review. Here are my findings.

---

## Scope & method

Reviewed `git show aab7e9b` plus the current working tree, the design critique `reports/deepseek_live_roster_design_2026-10-21.md` (its verdict is at line 929), and every consumer/insert site named in the brief. All operations were read-only (git show, grep, read).

---

## The 8 design-critique priority fixes vs. what was implemented

| # | Priority (report:929) | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Fail-closed on missing/stale/empty + distinct banner | ✅ implemented |
| 2 | Wire roster into `checkStandardLiveGates` (+ `pocRotationJoin`) | ✅ implemented |
| 3 | Fix `window_days` / unique-constraint / ON-CONFLICT | ✅ implemented |
| 4 | Roster check alongside `CAPITAL_EXPOSURE_OVERRIDE` so `SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED` can't bypass | ⚠️ **partially** — see item 3 |
| 5 | Keep `MANUAL_SUPPRESS_OVERRIDE` in force, document 4-live/2-shadow | ✅ implemented |
| 6 | Promotion-only hysteresis (leave-one-day-out CI) | ✅ implemented |
| 7 | Reuse `family_preview`'s exact filter (not `REAL_TRADE_FILTER`) + reconcile POC exclusion | ✅ implemented |
| 8 | Distinct roster reason in `orderEligibility.js` + audit `levelApproach.js` | ✅ implemented (levelApproach side effect noted below) |

---

## Focus item 1 — `isLiveEligible` is sync, reads `_rosterCache`; null/stale cache and the test hook

**Verdict: PASS.**

- Null cache **blocks** (fail-closed). `rosterBlockReason` at `setupEligibility.js:134-139` returns `'LIVE_ROSTER_UNAVAILABLE'` when `_rosterCache.val` is null or `state !== 'OK'` (`:137`). `isLiveEligible` calls it first at `:428`, ahead of the `SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED` short-circuit, so the null-cache case cannot wrongly allow — it wrongly *blocks*, which is safe.
- No production caller can reach `isLiveEligible` before the cache is loaded. Its only production callers are `acd.js:4737`, `:6628`, `:7007`, all of which execute after `computeSuppressionSets(...)` at `acd.js:4163`, which calls `getLiveRoster()` → populates `_rosterCache` (`setupEligibility.js:262-264`).
- The 60s cache (`ROSTER_CACHE_MS=60_000`, `:112`) is harmless for a roster that changes at most once per day. A stale cache can only keep a *just-demoted* type live for ≤60s at the daily cron boundary (a promotion-gate, not a leak of never-roster types); and because `checkLiveRoster` runs on every detector poll, `orderEligibility`, `live-flags`, and `getCanonicalLiveStatus`, the cache refreshes ~continuously in practice. No "wrongly allow a non-roster type" case exists.
- `__setLiveRosterForTest` (`:132`, `at = Date.now()+3_600_000`) is imported/called **only** by `scripts/test_live_roster.mjs`, a standalone process that exits. It is not referenced by any server code (`search_codebase` found only the test + the request doc). The 1h-future `at` pinning cannot leak outside a test process. It's a fragile test hook (a module-level mutable with a 1h pin), but contained.

---

## Focus item 2 — `rosterBlocked` into `suppressedSetups` only; `_trueSuppressedSetups`/THIN_N backfill not polluted

**Verdict: PASS.**

- `computeSuppressionSets` adds roster-blocked types to `suppressedSetups` **and** a new `rosterBlocked` set only (`setupEligibility.js:261-266`), never `knownTypes`.
- acd.js's `_trueSuppressedSetups` is built **independently** from the raw SETUP_STATUS rows, not from `computeSuppressionSets`'s return: `acd.js:4172-4174` (`setupStatusQ.rows.filter(r => r.recommendation === 'SUPPRESS')`). So it is **not** polluted — exactly what report line 888 demanded.
- The early-touch backfill loop's skip check (`acd.js:5482`) reads `liveStats._trueSuppressedSetups`, so off-roster THIN_N types are still backfilled. Correct.
- Note: `acd.js:4163` destructures only `{suppressedSetups, dowSuppressToday, knownTypes}` (it ignores `rosterBlocked`), and assigns `liveStats._suppressedSetups = suppressedSetups` (`:4164`) — so roster-blocked types *are* in `_suppressedSetups`, which is what makes the primary `_suppressedSetups.has()` reads roster-safe too.
- `orderEligibility.js:69-71` checks `rosterBlocked?.has(setupType)` *before* the generic `suppressedSetups` check, so it emits the distinct reason. ✅
- `test_invariants.mjs` has **no** check reading `suppressedSetups`/`rosterBlocked`/`computeSuppressionSets` (only an unrelated bet_class "roster cap" check at `:1551-1568`). No invariant breakage.
- **Side effect to note (not a bug):** `levelApproach.js:60` + `:121` use `suppressedSetups.has(setupType)` to set `isSuppressed`/`decisive`. Adding ~265 off-roster types makes them `decisive=false`, so the "likely to fire today" recommendation population shrinks to roster types only. That is consistent with the gate's intent (off-roster types can't go live anyway), but it *is* the behavior change the report (line 892) said to "confirm acceptable."

---

## Focus item 3 — any INSERT path that can still produce `origin_status='ACTIVE'` off-roster

**Verdict: PASS today, with one real latent gap.**

Roster-gated (verified) ACTIVE-capable sites:

| Site | Gate |
|---|---|
| acd.js main RTH insert `:6746` | `isLiveEligible` at `:6628` → `rosterBlockReason` |
| acd.js shadowCandidates `:7088` | `isLiveEligible` at `:7007` |
| acd.js cluster sibling `:4778` | `isLiveEligible` at `:4737` |
| acd.js STACK_VOL_BREAK `:3220` | `getStackVolBreakLiveStatus` → `getCanonicalLiveStatus` (`:389`) → `checkLiveRoster` |
| acd.js Globex `:1024` | `getOvernightLevelLiveStatus` → `getCanonicalLiveStatus` (`:374`) → roster check |
| `globexFlush`/`rthFlush`/`minuteBar` | `checkStandardLiveGates` → `checkLiveRoster` (`detectorLiveGates.js:50`) |
| `pocRotationJoinDetector` | `checkLiveRoster` (`:87`) |
| `ibLowPnr`/`majorPivot`/`minorDefendedLevel`/`stallDefendedLevel`/`overnightOrderflowEntry` | `getCanonicalLiveStatus` (roster-gated) |
| `momentumChaseDetector` | hardcoded `'SHADOW','SHADOW'` (`:148`) — never ACTIVE |
| `acdRiskGates.js` | no INSERT (only a comment) |

**The gap:** the two `SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED` promotion paths use `suppressAllDisabledOverrides()` (`setupEligibility.js:416-418`), which checks **only** `SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED.enabled && !CAPITAL_EXPOSURE_OVERRIDE.has(type)` — **no roster check**:

- Near-level suppressed-audit: `acd.js:5230` (`auditCanPromote = ... && suppressAllDisabledOverrides(type)` → `auditStatus='ACTIVE'` at `:5231`).
- Early-touch backfill: `acd.js:6149` (`btCanPromote = suppressAllDisabledOverrides(bt.type)` → ACTIVE at `:6164-6165`).

Because off-roster types are folded into `suppressedSetups`, `classifySuppressionReason` collapses them to `SUPPRESSED_FADE` (which is in `PROMOTABLE_SUPPRESS_REASONS`), so under a re-enabled `SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED` these two branches would promote off-roster types to `origin_status='ACTIVE'`, bypassing the roster. This is **dormant today** (`SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED.enabled === false`, `:392`), but it is precisely the "a global override silently misses some insert sites" failure class this codebase has hit repeatedly (GLOBEX_PAUSED, FLOOR_S3) and exactly what design-critique priority #4 was meant to prevent. It is the one place the commit's claim "all priority fixes applied" is incomplete.

---

## Focus item 4 — `calibrate_live_roster.mjs` correctness

**Verdict: PASS.**

- `evaluateType` (`:31-45`) matches `family_preview_20261021.mjs` exactly (thin at `n<20 || days<10`; `pass = ci.lo>0 && h1>0 && h2>0`). `dayBlockedBootstrapCI`/`collapseClusterSiblings` are called with the default `date`/`cluster_touch_id` fields and correct `seedKey` (`rigorDiagnostics.js:220,245`).
- `leave-best-day-out` (`:38-44`) drops the single highest-total-pnl day and recomputes the CI. Minor note: the `rest.length >= MIN_EVENTS` guard (`:42`) means a type at exactly N=20 can never survive the test (dropping a day drops it below 20) — a slightly conservative hysteresis, not a bug.
- `buildRoster` (`:48-60`) implements hysteresis correctly: first run (`prevPassing===null`) seeds without it; new promotions require `passLeaveBestDayOut`; demotion is instant; siblings are added only when `known.has(sib)`.
- `oppositeOf` (`:24-28`) is correct against the real naming conventions. `_LONG`/`_SHORT` always appear at end-of-string or before `_` (`*_OVERNIGHT`, `*_GAP_UP/DOWN`, `*_V2`, `TRT_MAH_*`, `GLOBEX_FLUSH_REVERSAL_LONG`), never mid-word; the lookahead replace preserves the suffix; and the `known.has(sib)` guard drops any nonexistent flip. The "GLOBEX_FLUSH_REVERSAL_LONG" concern is unfounded — `_LONG` is terminal there.
- Unique-key INSERT (`:83-85`): `window_days=0`, `ON CONFLICT (run_date, window_days, signal_type, signal_name) DO UPDATE` matches the real constraint `schema.sql:10921`.
- Previous-roster lookup `run_date < CURRENT_DATE` (`:72`) correctly excludes today's own (possibly re-run/overwritten) row.
- Lookahead/day-boundary: the population query has only a lower bound (`trade_date >= '2026-07-09'`), so it includes any already-resolved rows from the current ET session when run after close — normal for a daily cron. `CURRENT_DATE` is used consistently by both writer and reader for `run_date`/`age`; the ET-vs-server-local question is codebase-wide, not introduced here.
- No empty/partial write on transient error: any query/INSERT throw rejects `main()` → `process.exit(1)`; the INSERT is a single atomic statement. A legitimately-empty roster writes `roster=[]`, which the reader maps to `EMPTY` → fail-closed (correct).

---

## Focus item 5 — fail-closed on DB error in `getLiveRoster`

**Verdict: PASS.**

`getLiveRoster` (`setupEligibility.js:118-127`) wraps the query and JSON parse in `try/catch`; any throw yields `{ state:'MISSING', types:new Set(), runDate:null }`. `rosterBlockReason` then returns `'LIVE_ROSTER_UNAVAILABLE'` → blocked. There is no path where a DB error yields anything other than MISSING/blocked. Minor note: the `catch (_)` (`:127`) swallows the error silently (no logging), but the MISSING state is still surfaced via `live-flags` → `liveRosterState` and the quick-check banner, so it's observable.

---

## Focus item 6 — verdict

**Ship with one change.**

The gate is correctly fail-closed, correctly wired to every primary ACTIVE-insert path, the calibration math and the DB key/ON-CONFLICT are correct, and six of the eight design-critique priorities are fully and correctly implemented. The single real miss is a latent roster bypass:

> **Close the `suppressAllDisabledOverrides` roster gap.** Either (a) add a roster check inside `suppressAllDisabledOverrides` in `setupEligibility.js:416-418` (make it `SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED.enabled && !CAPITAL_EXPOSURE_OVERRIDE.has(setupType) && !rosterBlockReason(setupType)`), or (b) add `rosterBlockReason`/`checkLiveRoster` at the two call sites `acd.js:5230` and `acd.js:6149` before they promote to `'ACTIVE'`. Without this, a future re-enable of `SUPPRESS_ALL_DISABLED` would promote off-roster types to `origin_status='ACTIVE'` through the near-level-audit and early-touch-backfill branches, exactly the "global override silently misses an insert site" bug class the rest of this commit exists to eliminate.

**Two non-blocking notes to record (no code change required to ship):**
1. `levelApproach.js` recommendation population now excludes off-roster types (intended, but it is the side effect the design review flagged "confirm acceptable" — I confirm it's acceptable).
2. `leave-best-day-out`'s `rest.length >= MIN_EVENTS` guard makes the hysteresis marginally stricter at the N=20 boundary, and `getLiveRoster`'s error path is unlogged (MISSING is still surfaced via the banner).
